             Table 2 – Sacrificial Thickness to be Allowed on Each Surface of                                         Table
                      Galvanised Steel Exposed to Corrosion in Selected Fill                 of

                                                           Sacrificial Thickness (mm)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents      Design Life (years)
                               Non-Submerged Structure        Submerged Structure
             10                          0                          0
             50                            0.30                           0.55
             60                            0.38                           0.63
             70                            0.45                           0.70
            120                            0.75                           1.00                                        Table
                                                                        of
   Notes:     (1) Values based on BS 8006 (BSI, 1995).
               (2) Galvanised steel shall comply with Section 4.1.5(4).
               (3) These sacrificial thicknesses apply to steels embedded in selected fill complying                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
                 with the requirements stated in Clause A.15 of the Model Specification in
               Appendix A.  Sites or fill materials of special aggressiveness should be assessed
                   separately.
               (4) A sacrificial thickness of 0.5 times the values quoted should be deducted from
                each internal face of all component parts in close metal-to-metal contact or
                 wholly enclosed within the connection.
               (5) Submerged structure means structure that is periodically submerged in water
                  but excluding marine conditions and contaminated or saline water.                                            Table

                  Loading                        Ultimate Limit      Serviceability
                                                            State           Limit State

Water pressure                                             1.0                1.0

Contents
               (2)  The external loads to which the partial load factors are associated should be the

  Notes :      (1)  Load combination A: considers the maximum values of all loads and normally generates
                    the maximum reinforcement tension and foundation bearing pressure.
               (2)  Load combination B: considers the maximum overturning loads from the retained
                 ground together with the minimum self weight of the structure and superimposed traffic
                    load. This combination usually dictates the reinforcement requirement for pullout                               Table
                    resistance and is usually the worst case for sliding along the base.                      of
               (3)  Load combination C: considers dead load only with unit partial load factors. This
                 combination is used to determine foundation settlements and reinforcement tension for
                  checking the serviceability limit state.                   GLOSSARY OF TERMS                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
Reinforced fill         A.07     Reinforced  fill structure  is a structure with a vertical or near-
structure                              vertical facing that is within 20º from the vertical which comprises
                                        tensile reinforcing elements embedded in a compacted mass of fill
                               and shall include any connections, facings and granular filter and
                                   drainage material which may be necessary to ensure its stability.

Reinforced fill slope    A.08     Reinforced fill slope is a feature with a face inclination of more
                                   than 20º from the  vertical which comprises tensile  reinforcing                Table
                                  elements embedded in a compacted mass of fill and shall include       of
                               any connections, facings and granular filter and drainage material
                              which may be necessary to ensure its stability.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents

196
Fill material          A.09       Fill material is the material in the reinforced fill structure or slope                Table
                                      in contact with the reinforcing elements, connections and facings,      of
                               and shall include both the selected fill material and any granular
                                              filter and drainage material.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
Selected fill material    A.10      Selected  fill  material  is  that  part  of the  fill material  in  the
                                    reinforced fill structure or slope which is not primarily provided as
                                 a filter or for drainage.

Reinforced fill product  A.11     Reinforced fill product certificate is a certificate issued by the Civil

Fill material          A.15      (1)     Fill  material  shall  consist  of  naturally  occurring  or                Table
                                  processed material which at the time of deposition is capable of      of
                                  being compacted in accordance with the specified requirements to
                               form a stable mass of fill.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
                                      (2)     Fill material shall not contain any of the following:

                                                (a)   material  susceptible  to volume  change,  including
                                            marine mud, swelling clays and collapsible soils,

                                              (b)   peat, vegetation, timber, organic, soluble or perishable
                                                   material,                                                                                        Table
                                                (c)  dangerous or toxic material or material susceptible to      of
                                             combustion, and
                                              (d)   metal, rubber, plastic or synthetic material.                                                        Contents

                                      (3)   The grading and index properties of the selected fill shall be
                                      in accordance with the requirement specified in Table A.1.

                                      (4)    Selected fill for reinforced fill structures or slopes which
                                   contain hot-dip galvanized steel reinforcing elements shall comply
                                  with the electrical and chemical limits specified in Table A.2.                          Table
                                      (5)    Materials from excavation shall not be used as fill material      of
                                      for a reinforced  fill structure or slope unless permitted by the
                                   Engineer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
                                      (6)     Fill material shall meet any additional requirements given in
                                      the Drawings or in the reinforced fill product certificate.

Handling and storage   A.22     Facing units shall be stored and handled in such a manner as toof facing units                      eliminate the possibility of any damage. They shall be stored flat                          Contents
                               and supported on firm blocking. The use of porous blocks to stack
                                    facing units shall be avoided.

Handling and storage   A.23      (1)    Reinforcing elements  shall  not  be  subjected  to  rough
of reinforcing elements            handling, shock loading or dropping from a height.

                                      (2)    Reinforcing elements shall be stored in such a manner to                                                                                                                                                                                     Table                                    eliminate the possibility of any damage and shall be clearly labeled
                                      to identify items with different dimensions and properties.             of

                                      (3)   Nylon, rope or padded slings  shall be used  for  lifting                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents                                   galvanized reinforcing elements; bundles of reinforcement shall be
                                          lifted with a strongback or with multiple supports to prevent
                                   abrasion or excessive bending.

                                      (4)    Polymeric reinforcing elements shall be properly stored and
                                    protected from precipitation, extend  ultraviolet radiation,  direct
                                       sunlight, chemicals that are strong acids or strong bases, flames
                                    including welding sparks, temperature in excess of 50°C, and any                                                                                                                                                                                     Table
                                    other environmental  condition  that may damage  the  physical                                                                        of
                                   property values.

Handling and storage   A.24      (1)     Fill material shall not be handled or stored in a manner                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contentsof fill material                  which will result in segregation, deterioration, erosion or instability
                                   of the material.

                                      (2)    Different types of fill material shall be kept separate from
                                each other.  Fill material shall not be contaminated and shall be
                                  maintained in a suitable condition for deposition and compaction.
                                                                                                                                                                                     Table
                  FOUNDATION PREPARATION                      of

Foundation           A.25      (1)    Unless otherwise specified by the Engineer,  all  existing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contentspreparation                        vegetation and all unsuitable foundation material shall be removed
                                      in those areas where the reinforcing element is to be placed.

                                      (2)    Surfaces on which reinforcing elements are to be placed
                                       shall be uniform, smooth and free of abrupt changes in slope,
                                    debris  and  irregularities  that  could  damage  the  reinforcing
                                   elements.

                                      (3)   During periods of heavy rainfall, the Contractor shall be      of

                                      sheeting.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents

                                      (4)    Surface  water  flowing  over  exposed  surfaces  of  the                Table

                   ERECTION OF FACING

Erection of elemental   A.26      (1)    Elemental facing units shall be placed in successive courses
facing                             unless otherwise specified.  The spacing, level and alignment of                Table                                each unit shall be checked immediately after its placement and
                                  again at the completion of each course.                        of

                                      (2)   Adequate support of the facing units shall be provided at                                each stage of erection. The bottom course of facing units shall be                          Contents
                                 shored to prevent movement during the deposition and compaction
                                   of fill material.

                                      (3)   As placed, all elemental facing units except those at the
                               bottom course shall be inclined towards the fill to compensate for
                                outward movement expected during or subsequent to compaction
                                   of                                         the                                                              fill material.                                              The degree                                                                    of inclination                                                                                            shall                                                                             be adjusted
                               where                                         necessary                                                       as deposition                                                           and                                                                compaction                                                                                    of  fill                                                                                              material                Table
                                 proceeds to ensure that the tolerances specified in Clause A.35 are      of
                                  met.
Erection of full height  A.27      (1)    Full height facings shall be properly placed and propped                          Contents
facing                             during construction. The level and alignment of each facing shall
                               be checked immediately  after  its placement and again at the
                                 completion of  filling.  The foundation for the props  shall be
                                 adequate to support the propping loads.

                                      (2)   The degree of inclination of the full height facing shall be
                                     adjusted, and  the stage when the props  are removed shall be                Table                                   defined to ensure that the tolerances specified in Clause A.35 are
                                  met.                                              of

                  PLACEMENT OF REINFORCING ELEMENTS                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  ContentsPlacement and        A.29      (1)   The reinforcing elements shall be placed on the compacted
connection of                                fill material and connected to the facing units in accordance with
reinforcing elements                the Drawings.  They shall be placed at right angles to the facing
                                       units or the plan face of the slope, unless otherwise shown in the
                                  drawings.   Bends in  steel reinforcing elements  shall be to a
                           minimum radius of 300 mm.
                                      (2)   For reinforced fill slopes, in which the overfill and cut back                Table
                                   technique  is proposed to ensure proper compaction of the slope      of
                                      face, the construction method  shall ensure that the reinforcing
                                  elements are exposed on the final slope face.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
                                      (3)    Polymeric  reinforcing elements  shall be pulled  tight  to
                                    eliminate waves and wrinkles and secured in place as necessary by
                                        staples, pins, sand bags, backfill or as directed by the Engineer
                                        after placement.

                                      (4)    After a layer of polymeric reinforcing element has been
                                    placed, the next succeeding layer of fill material shall be placed
                               and compacted as soon as practicable to avoid potential damage or                                                                                                                                                                                     Table
                                 extended exposure to direct sunlight.  No polymeric reinforcing      of
                                  elements  shall  be  left exposed  for more than 8  hours  after
                                 placement unless approved by the Engineer.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents                                      (5)    Unless otherwise specified in the Drawings or as approved
                             by the Engineer, no splices or seams shall be made in the primary
                                     direction of tensile strength in the polymeric reinforcing elements.
                         When splices are approved, they shall be made for the full width of
                                      the polymeric reinforcing elements by using a similar material with
                                     similar strength.  Splices shall not be placed within 1.5 m of the
                                    facing unit or slope face, within 1.5 m below top of structure or
                                      slope, nor within 1.5 m horizontally adjacent to another splice.                                                                                                                                                                                     Table

                    DEPOSITION AND COMPACTION OF FILL                  MATERIAL                                                                                                                                                           Contents

Deposition and        A.30      (1)     Fill shall be deposited and compacted in near-horizontal
compaction of fill                   layers of the thicknesses required to achieve the specified end
material                          product and shall, as far as practicable, be brought up at a uniform
                                       rate so that all parts of the Site reach finished (formation) level at
                                      the same time.
                                      (2)   The  fill material beyond 1.5 m of the back face of the                Table
                                      structure or slope may be raised in thicker layers than that within      of
                                      the  1.5 m zone  provided  that  this  is  compatible  with  the
                                 arrangement of the  reinforcing elements and  the  difference in                               compacted levels does not exceed 300 mm.                                                                                       Contents

                                      (3)   The  fill material shall be deposited, spread, levelled and
                               compacted in layers of thickness appropriate to the compaction
                               methods to be used and so that each reinforcing element can be
                                     fixed at the required level on top of the compacted fill material
                                   without any voids forming  directly underneath the  reinforcing
                                   element. Unless otherwise permitted by the Engineer, layers of fill
                                    material shall be horizontal, except for any gradient required for                Table
                                     drainage, and the thickness of each layer shall be uniform over the       of
                                   area to be filled.

                                      (4)   The deposition and compaction of  fill material shall be                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
                                     carried out in a direction parallel to the face of the structure or
                                   slope and  shall be completed in stages  to follow closely  the
                                     erection of facing units and the deposition of reinforcing elements.

                                      (5)   The fill material shall be compacted as soon as practicable
                                        after being deposited and in a manner appropriate to the location
                               and to the material to be compacted. The in situ dry density of the
                               compacted fill material shall be at least 95% of the maximum dry                Table
                                      density.  Compaction shall continue until the whole layer of fill      of
                                    material has attained the minimum in situ dry density specified
                                  above.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents


===== PDF PAGE 208 =====
207
                                      (6)    Cobbles, boulders, rock or waste fragments whose largest                Table
                                dimension is greater than two-thirds of the loose layer thickness      of
                                       shall not be incorporated into the fill.
                                      (7)   The Contractor shall ensure that the reinforcing elements                          Contents
                               and facing units are not damaged or displaced during deposition
                               and compaction.   Tracked machines or vehicles  shall not be
                                  operated on top of reinforcing elements which are not covered by
                                        at least 150 mm of fill material.

                                      (8)   No fill shall be deposited and left uncompacted at the end of
                                 a                                  working                                             day.                                          The                                                          surface                                                                of                                                           compacted                                                                                                                       fill                                                                                platform shall be                Table                                 graded                                              to ensure                                                        free                                                         runoff                                                              of                                                                  rainwater                                                                         without                                                                                 ponding.

Moisture content of fill  A.31      (1)   The  fill material shall be  at optimum moisture content
material                          during compaction.  The  tolerance on the optimum moisture                                    content shall be ±3% provided that the fill material is capable of                          Contents
                                  being compacted in accordance with the specified requirement to
                               form a stable mass of fill. All necessary measures shall be taken to
                                  achieve and maintain the specified moisture content. The moisture
                                    content of the compacted surfaces shall be controlled to prevent
                                  cracking due to drying.

                                      (2)                                  The                                              Contractor                                                                shall                                                                take all                                                                     necessary                                                                                    steps                                                                                             to                                                                                      ensure that
                                      the fill                                                         is deposited                                                             at the                                                           moisture                                                                      content                                                                           necessary                                                                                            to                                                                                     achieve                                                                                                      the                Table
                                     specified level of compaction and  shall, where necessary, add       of
                                 water to or dry the fill, in order to obtain this value. Where it is
                                  necessary to add water, this shall be done as a fine spray and in                                such a way that there is time for the water to be absorbed into the                          Contents
                                                   fill before being rolled by the plant.

                                      (3)   The Contractor shall examine the deposited fill and remove
                               any deteriorated material prior to recommencement of filling.

Compaction plant      A.32      (1)    All vehicles and all construction equipment weighing more
                                   than 1000 kg shall be kept at least 1.5 m away from the face of the
                                      structure or slope.                                                                                              Table
                                                                        of
                                      (2)   Compaction plant and compaction method shall be selected
                                 having regard to the proximity of existing trenches, excavations,
                                      retaining walls or other structures and all work shall be performed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents
                                      in such a way as to ensure that  their existing stability  is not
                                   impaired.  In particular great care should be taken to limit the
                                compactive effort close to reinforced fill facing panels to prevent
                             damage to connections or produce displacement of the facing.

                                      (3)    Unless otherwise permitted by the Engineer, the fill material
                                   within                                          1.5                         m                                                  of the                                                            face                                                              of                                                                    reinforced                                                                                                                   fill structures or slopes                Table                                  supported                                      by                                                   facings                                                              shall                                                     be                                                        compacted                                                                                using:

Compaction adjacent   A.33     During construction, the  fill material retained at the rear of the                Table
to structures                       reinforced fill block, defined as the position coinciding with the       of

                     TESTING: FILL MATERIAL –

Testing: shear strength A.57     The shear strength of the fill material shall be determined using
                                          triaxial  apparatus or shear box apparatus  in accordance  with
                              Geospec 3. For shear strength test using shear box apparatus, the                                                                                                                                                                                     Table                                          test specimen shall be sheared under drained conditions under a
                                normal  stress equal to the  theoretical maximum vertical earth      of
                                    pressure in the reinforced fill structures or slopes.

                     TESTING: COEFFICIENT OF FRICTION
                  BETWEEN FILL MATERIAL AND                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents                  REINFORCEMENT – DIRECT SLIDING

Testing: direct sliding   A.61     The  coefficient  of  friction between  the   fill  material and  the
                                     reinforcing elements shall be determined by the direct shear test in
                                 accordance with Section 16.2 of Geospec 3, with the following
                                    modification:

                                               (a)  The weight  of  fill material required  to prepare a                                                                                                                                                                                     Table                                         compacted test specimen 300 mm x 300 mm x 75 mm
                                                    shall be calculated.                             of

                                             (b)  For strip reinforcing elements, the strips shall be cut to                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents                                                       tightly fit the interior plan shape of the lower half of


===== PDF PAGE 217 =====
216
                                                  the shear box.  Ribbed strips shall be cut so that the                Table
                                                     ribs can be placed as far away from the edge of the       of
                                       box as possible. For plane strips, the top surface and
                                                   for ribbed  strips, the surface defined by the plane                                             through the root of the ribs, shall be at least 1 mm and                          Contents
                                                not more than 3 mm below the top edge of the lower
                                                    half of the shear box. The reinforcing elements shall
                                          be aligned so that shearing can occur in a direction
                                                     parallel to their longitudinal axes. The strips shall then
                                          be placed and secured in the lower shear box by filling
                                                  the lower shear box with plaster of Paris so that the
                                                        strips remain fixed at all stages of the test.                                     Table
                                               (c)  For grid, sheet or mesh reinforcing elements, the  fill      of
                                                material shall be compacted into the lower shear box
                                                   in accordance with Clause 16.2.5 of Geospec 3, except                                                      that the surface of the second compacted layer shall be                          Contents
                                         between 1 mm and 2 mm below the top edge of the
                                            lower shear box. The grid, sheet or mesh shall then be
                                                cut and fitted to match the width of the shear box and
                                                   to allow it to be secured over the top edge of the lower
                                               shear box. The reinforcing elements shall be aligned
                                           so that shearing can occur in a direction parallel to
                                                       their longitudinal axes.                                                                   Table
                                             (d)  The   fill  material  shall  be  deposited  over  the      of
                                                 reinforcing element and compacted:  in two  equal
                                                  layers until about 20 mm of the compacted fill projects                                         above the top edge of the upper box,  if vibratory                          Contents
                                          compaction is used; or in two equal layers until the top
                                               of the compacted surface  is approximately 20 mm
                                        below the top of the shear box, if static compaction is
                                               used.

                                               (e)  Shearing  shall  be  carried out  until  the  horizontal
                                              displacement is twice the displacement recorded at
                                         peak shear stress or until any rib comes into contact                Table
                                             with the edge of the shear box, whichever occurs first.       of

                                                      (f)  The result of the test shall be taken as the maximum
                                                      ratio between the shear stress and the normal stress.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  Contents

===== PDF PAGE 236 =====
235
                                                                                                                                                                                     Table                GLOSSARY OF TERMS
                                                                        of

Design load.  The load obtained by factoring the selected value by a partial load factor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 Contents
Design strength.   The strength of material obtained by factoring the characteristic strength
       by a partial material factor.

Design tension.   The tensile force developed in the reinforcement due to the design load
        acting on the reinforced fill structure or slope.
Geogrid.   Polymeric, planar structure consisting of an open network of connected tensile                Table
        elements used in geotechnical and civil engineering applications.                     of

Geotextile.    Permeable, polymeric material, which may be woven, nonwoven or knitted,       used in geotechnical and civil engineering applications.                                                                                                               Contents

Partial consequence factor.    The factor applied to the design value of a reinforcement
        parameter or a parameter relating to the interaction between facing units.

Partial load factor.  The factor applied to the selected value of a loading.
Partial material factor.  The factor applied to the selected value of a geotechnical parameter                Table        or a reinforcement parameter or a parameter relating to the interaction between
        facing units.                                                          of

Polymeric reinforcement.   The generic term that encompasses geosynthetic reinforcement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 Contents        materials used in geotechnical engineering.

Proprietary products.   Manufactured proprietary materials used in reinforced fill structures
        or slopes including reinforcement, facings, connections and fasteners,  filters and
         drains, joint fillers and sealants.

Reinforced fill.   Compacted mass of fill with predominantly horizontal layered reinforcing
        elements to improve its tensile and shear strength capacities.                                                            Table
                                                                        of
Reinforced fill product certificate.  A certificate issued by the Civil Engineering Department
        of the Hong Kong SAR Government, which specifies suitable design strengths of a         specific proprietary reinforcement and the conditions of use for the Hong Kong                          Contents
        conditions.

Reinforced fill slope.  A slope with a face inclination of more than 20º from the vertical
       which comprises tensile reinforcing elements embedded in a compacted mass of fill
       and shall include any connections, facings and granular filter and drainage material
       which may be necessary to ensure its stability.
                                                                                                                                                                                     TableReinforced fill structure.  A structures with a vertical or near-vertical facing that is within
        20º from the vertical which comprises tensile reinforcing elements embedded in a       of
       compacted mass of fill and shall include any connections, facings and granular filter
       and drainage material which may be necessary to ensure its stability.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 Contents


===== PDF PAGE 237 =====
236
                                                                                                                                                                                     Table
Residual soil.    Soil derived from insitu rock weathering in which all trace of the original                                                                        of
        rock texture, fabric and structure has been destroyed.

Saprolite.   Soil derived from insitu rock weathering in which evidence of the original rock                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 Contents         texture, fabric and structure is retained.

Serviceability limit state.  A state at which movements of the reinforced fill structure or
        slope affect the appearance or efficient use of the structure or slope or nearby
         structures or services which rely upon its support.
Ultimate limit state.  A state at which a failure mechanism can form in the ground or within                Table        or through the reinforced fill structure or slope, or when movement of the reinforced
            fill structure or slope leads to severe damage to its structural elements or in nearby       of
         structures or services.                                                                                                                                                                                                             Contents
